{\footnotesize\ttfamily static void snd\+\_\+soc\+\_\+dapm\+\_\+init\+\_\+bias\+\_\+level (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsnd__soc__dapm__context}{snd\+\_\+soc\+\_\+dapm\+\_\+context}} $\ast$}]{dapm,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{interfaceenum}{enum}} \mbox{\hyperlink{soc-dapm_8h_ad12e14c84a378dc7f97333dfe6542481}{snd\+\_\+soc\+\_\+bias\+\_\+level}}}]{level }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

\mbox{\hyperlink{soc-dapm_8h_a42e368ef9172dfa01515aad6e714a2fe}{snd\+\_\+soc\+\_\+dapm\+\_\+init\+\_\+bias\+\_\+level()}} -\/ Initialize D\+A\+PM bias level @dapm\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} D\+A\+PM context to initialize @level\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} D\+A\+PM level to initialize to

This function only sets the driver internal state of the D\+A\+PM level and will not modify the state of the device. Hence it should not be used during normal operation, but only to synchronize the internal state to the device state. E.\+g. during driver probe to set the D\+A\+PM level to the one corresponding with the power-\/on reset state of the device.

To change the D\+A\+PM state of the device use snd\+\_\+soc\+\_\+dapm\+\_\+set\+\_\+bias\+\_\+level(). Here is the caller graph for this function\+:
% FIG 4
